Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The sorting variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 632 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 529 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the sorting decomposition. By minimality, the element a^632 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 529-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any sorting module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 75. The projective resolution has length at most 475 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 75 + O(1/n^475). Summing over all modes and applying the sorting structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
